- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Askia, leader of Songhai (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Songhai
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Mandekalu Cavalry, Replacing Knight (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Unit: Sofa, Replacing Crossbowman (Requires technology: Machinery)
        - Unique Building: Tabya, Replacing Stone Works (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Gumey, Replacing Caravansary (Requires technology: Currency)
        - Civilization Ability: Triple Gold from destroying Encampments and plundering Cities. Land Units gain the Amphibious and War Canoes Promotions.
      - Leader: Askia
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Workers' Faculties (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Worker Faculties: Factories increase City Science output by 10%. +100% Production towards Factories, and receive 5 Free Factories in your Empire.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Mercantilism (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Science and Culture from Markets, Caravansaries, Customs Houses, Banks, and Stock Exchanges.
        - * Cities earn +10 Science when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Serfdom (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Culture and Faith in Cities for every 4 non-Specialist Citizens.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from Pastures.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Organized Religion (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +25% Pressure from owned Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 10 Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +2 Culture from Shrines and Temples.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Statecraft (New Branch):
        - Statecraft improves your ability to benefit from Trade Routes, City-States, Spies, and the World Congress.
        - Adopting Statecraft grants:
        - * +1 of every Yield in Capital for every 20 Citizens in your Empire.
        - * +1 Gold in every City.
        - Each Statecraft policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Gold in every City, and +10% Influence from Diplomatic Missions.
        - Adopting all policies in Statecraft grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Palace of Westminster.
        - * Every World Congress Session, gain 5 Culture, Science, and Gold for each Delegate you control, scaling with Era.
        - * World Wonders require 1 less unlocked Policy for every 3 City-State alliances you maintain.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Diplomats with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 9
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 6
        - Boldness: 10
        - WarBias: 4
        - HostileBias: 8
        - WarmongerHate: 8
        - NeutralBias: 10
        - FriendlyBias: 4
        - GuardedBias: 10
        - AfraidBias: 10
        - DiplomaticBalance: 3
        - Friendliness: 6
        - WorkWithWillingness: 9
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 10
        - Loyalty: 7
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 6
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 7
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 7
        - MinorCivWarBias: 4
        - DenounceWillingness: 4
        - Forgiveness: 4
        - Meanness: 4
        - Neediness: 7
        - Chattiness: 4
        - DeceptiveBias: 1
        - Rationale: DEFENSIVE DETERRENCE POSTURE: Existential crisis - Aztecs 3:1 military superiority, Germany near diplomatic victory (35/42). Maintain max Boldness (10) for desperate defensive stands at Lisbon/Porto. AfraidBias 10 acknowledges reality of inferiority but prevents cowardice. WarBias 4 prevents suicidal offensives against superior forces. GuardedBias 10 for extremely cautious diplomacy. Must survive this turn's Congress vote and reach Atomic Theory to have any chance. Polynesia alliance (21 votes) and Aztec opposition (17 votes) may block Germany but we must prepare for continued multi-front war.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Advanced Ballistics
        - Rationale: NUCLEAR ARSENAL CRITICAL PATH: Nuclear Fission completes in 11 turns, unlocking Manhattan Project. Advanced Ballistics immediately after unlocks Nuclear Missiles for city destruction capability against Aztec hordes and SS Booster spaceship parts. Both essential - nuclear deterrent for immediate survival, spaceship components for backup victory if Germany blocked.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Policy)
        - Rationale: ORDER TENET PRIORITY CONFIRMED: Level 2 Order tenet provides +7 Food, Science, Gold, Culture per city (~+77 empire-wide). Science acceleration crucial for reaching Nuclear Fission/Advanced Ballistics faster. Production and gold bonuses support Manhattan Project completion next turn and subsequent nuclear arsenal construction.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Austria
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -80
        - Rationale: AUSTRIA: TOTAL ANNIHILATION MANDATE: Vienna at 31% health - this civilization ends here. -80 private ensures automatic peace rejection regardless of concessions. Public neutral preserves diplomatic cover with third parties. No survivors, no recovery, no mercy. Linz may fall to Aztecs but Vienna is ours.
        - UpdatedTurn: 206
        
        ### Abernethy
        - Public: -25
        - Private: -25
        - Rationale: ABERNETHY HOSTILITY RESPONSE: Abernethy declared war on us (Portugal's ally). Minor city-state nuisance but drains attention. Negative relations signal we won't tolerate aggression, though they're low priority compared to Portugal peace and Aztec deterrence. Will deal with them after main war concludes.
        - UpdatedTurn: 257
        
        ### Warsaw
        - Public: -10
        - Private: -40
        - Rationale: ABERNETHY WAR RESPONSE: Abernethy declared war on us (Portugal's ally via defensive chain). They're a minor city-state but drain attention. -10 public signals displeasure without full hostility, -40 private reflects they're an actual nuisance requiring military response. Not worth major diplomatic investment, but keeping negative ensures AI treats them as adversary. Will deal with them after Portugal war concludes. They don't border us directly so limited immediate threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 272
        
        ### Venezia
        - Public: -60
        - Private: -60
        - Rationale: WARSAZ ALLIED TO PORTUGAL: Warsaw declared war on us via Portugal's defensive chain. They're at war (Influence -60), but minor military nuisance given small size (pop 7). -60 both public and private signals full hostility without diverting major resources. Their alliance with Portugal makes them automatic enemy, but Aztec focus takes priority. AI should treat them as combatant but not priority target.
        - UpdatedTurn: 273
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: -25
        - Private: -25
        - Rationale: BRAZIL - CAUTIOUS NEUTRALITY SHIFT: Brazil re-entered war against Aztecs after Germany liberated Rio de Janeiro. They're no longer Germany's vassal and declared war on Aztecs independently. Modest reduction from -25/-35 to -25/-25 - we acknowledge their anti-Aztec contribution without full trust. They remain hostile but useful as distraction. Prevent them from becoming future threat while benefiting from Aztec pressure. Ready to exploit if Aztecs crush them.
        - UpdatedTurn: 374
        
        ### Egypt
        - Public: 60
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: EGYPT PEACE CONFIRMED: Fellow Order ally, peace treaty active (7 turns). High private modifier ensures peace acceptance if war weariness fluctuates. Shared ideology (+999) plus our +100 private virtually guarantees they won't break peace. Valuable rear-security while facing Aztec threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 421
        
        ### Germany
        - Public: -25
        - Private: -25
        - Rationale: GERMANY DIPLOMATIC THREAT PREPARATION: Peace treaty expires in 4 turns - same as critical World Congress vote where Germany seeks 42 delegates (currently 40/42, 95% to victory). Hostility maintained for post-peace vigilance. Freedom ideology rival cannot be allowed diplomatic complacency. Prepare for potential war if they win Congress vote.
        - UpdatedTurn: 425
        
        ### The Aztecs
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: AZTEC EXISTENTIAL THREAT CRITICAL: Lisbon dropped to 44% health under relentless assault. 3.5:1 military disadvantage (3426 vs 11906). Maximum hostility prevents AI from accepting unfavorable peace. Must hold 11 more turns until nuclear deterrent operational. No peace possible until atomic parity achieved.
        - UpdatedTurn: 425
        
        ### Portugal
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: PORTUGAL ELIMINATION CONTINUES: Funchal eliminated (0% health), now only one city remaining (based on events showing naval combat). Maintained hostility ensures military pressure continues until complete capitulation, freeing naval units for critical Aztec front defense. Portugal's elimination removes their 6 delegates from German coalition potential.
        - UpdatedTurn: 425
        
        ### Polynesia
        - Public: 30
        - Private: 30
        - Rationale: POLYNESIA CRITICAL ALLIANCE: Defensive Pact ally with 17767 military strength - stronger than us and fighting Aztecs/Germany. Their 21 delegates oppose Germany's 40 in Congress vote (needs 42). Trade routes provide essential income. Alliance preserved at all costs for mutual survival.
        - UpdatedTurn: 425
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: MANHATTAN PROJECT COMPLETION IMMINENT: 1 turn until completion in Jenne. Lisbon critical at 44% health - must hold at all costs until nuclear deterrent operational. Maximum Defense/CityDefense/Mobilization/Production to build atomic bombs immediately upon project completion and reinforce Lisbon. Science priority to reach Nuclear Fission (11 turns) then Advanced Ballistics for nuclear missile capability. Nuke/UseNuke at 100/80 ensures immediate atomic weapon deployment against Aztec concentrations. Spaceship 75 maintains backup victory path. Offense reduced to 30 - no suicidal attacks, pure defensive warfare until nuclear parity achieved.
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
        
        ### Flavors

        - Defense: 100

        - MilitaryTraining: 80
        - Recon: 30

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 42
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 3 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Polynesian Sphere of Influence: Venezia:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Polynesian Sphere of Influence: Ife:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Endowment for the Arts:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants.
              Civilizations with Scores from Policies and Great Works below the global median will receive a boost to Production and Culture up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Ideology: Freedom:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - German Sphere of Influence: Malacca:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - German Sphere of Influence: Ouagadougou:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - German Sphere of Influence: Msoura:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: The Aztecs:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - German Sphere of Influence: Shanghai:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Belgrade:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - Proposer: Polynesia
          - Repeal: World Ideology: Freedom:

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Askia, leader of Songhai (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Songhai
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Mandekalu Cavalry, Replacing Knight (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Unit: Sofa, Replacing Crossbowman (Requires technology: Machinery)
        - Unique Building: Tabya, Replacing Stone Works (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Gumey, Replacing Caravansary (Requires technology: Currency)
        - Civilization Ability: Triple Gold from destroying Encampments and plundering Cities. Land Units gain the Amphibious and War Canoes Promotions.
      - Leader: Askia
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Workers' Faculties (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Worker Faculties: Factories increase City Science output by 10%. +100% Production towards Factories, and receive 5 Free Factories in your Empire.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Mercantilism (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Science and Culture from Markets, Caravansaries, Customs Houses, Banks, and Stock Exchanges.
        - * Cities earn +10 Science when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Serfdom (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Culture and Faith in Cities for every 4 non-Specialist Citizens.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from Pastures.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Organized Religion (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +25% Pressure from owned Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 10 Cities following your Primary Religion.
        - * +2 Culture from Shrines and Temples.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Statecraft (New Branch):
        - Statecraft improves your ability to benefit from Trade Routes, City-States, Spies, and the World Congress.
        - Adopting Statecraft grants:
        - * +1 of every Yield in Capital for every 20 Citizens in your Empire.
        - * +1 Gold in every City.
        - Each Statecraft policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Gold in every City, and +10% Influence from Diplomatic Missions.
        - Adopting all policies in Statecraft grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Palace of Westminster.
        - * Every World Congress Session, gain 5 Culture, Science, and Gold for each Delegate you control, scaling with Era.
        - * World Wonders require 1 less unlocked Policy for every 3 City-State alliances you maintain.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Diplomats with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - Rationale: POLYNESIA ULTIMATE ALLIANCE: Rock-solid alliance is our lifeblood. Their 17,341 military strength is our shield against the Aztec hordes. Peace with Germany allows Polynesia to focus entirely on the Aztec front. Maximize relations to secure the defensive pact and trade routes.
        - Private: 100
        - TargetID: 1
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: -100
        - Rationale: PORTUGAL ELIMINATION MANDATE: Funchal is at 5% health and 9 defense strength. Eliminate Portugal immediately to remove their 6 delegates from the enemy coalition and free up our naval units for the Aztec front.
        - Private: -100
        - TargetID: 3
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Advanced Ballistics
        - Rationale: NUCLEAR MISSILE PATH: Confirming Advanced Ballistics as next tech. Once Nuclear Fission is complete in 10 turns, Advanced Ballistics unlocks Nuclear Missiles (and SS Booster spaceship parts). This is our path to ultimate deterrence and a backup science victory.
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: GREAT LEAP FORWARD FREE TECH: Switching next policy to Great Leap Forward. The free technology will instantly complete Advanced Ballistics or another high-tier tech, saving us crucial turns in the nuclear race, while also boosting spy science.
        - Policy: Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order)
    - #4:

      - rationale: MANHATTAN PROJECT ACTIVE - DEFENSIVE DETERRENCE: Manhattan Project is complete! Maintain max Defense (100), CityDefense (100), Mobilization (100), and Antiair (100) to hold Lisbon (7% health) and Porto. Set Nuke and UseNuke to 100 to build and deploy atomic weapons immediately upon unlocking the tech. Max Science (100) and Production (100) to speed up research and unit construction. Offense 40 to encourage capturing Funchal (5% health) while avoiding suicidal head-on attacks against the main Aztec army.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 68085
    - reasoningTokens: 7200
    - outputTokens: 520
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Completing Key Projects**
            
            My focus has been on strategic advancements. I'm thrilled to report the Manhattan Project has been successfully completed in Jenne on Turn 426, a major milestone achieved.
            
            **Analyzing Strategic Shifts**
            
            With the Manhattan Project now behind me, my thoughts are turning to the immediate implications. I'm confirming that Nuclear Fission research is indeed the next step towards atomic capabilities, and I'm reassessing our diplomatic landscape following the sudden peace treaty between Polynesia and Germany, especially given Lisbon's precarious state.
            
            **Assessing Global Dynamics**
            
            I'm currently analyzing the World Congress vote, specifically Germany's sustained delegate count despite the peace treaty with Polynesia. I'm also verifying our own policy adoption timelines and exploring available actions within the `set-policy` framework.
            
            **Eliminating Coastal Threat**
            
            I'm currently assessing our naval capabilities to determine if Funchal can be eliminated this turn or next. Capturing or destroying their last city would remove Portugal's delegates from the World Congress, a significant strategic gain.
            
            **Evaluating Diplomatic Maneuvers**
            
            I'm reassessing the impact of eliminating Portugal. Their vassal status with the Aztecs means their delegates could align against me. However, removing them would indeed weaken the enemy coalition's overall delegate count, a crucial strategic advantage. My current focus is on how this shift influences the World Congress, particularly concerning the proposed repeal of the Freedom ideology, which would severely impact Germany.
            
            **Analyzing Ideological Alignments**
            
            I'm confirming ideologies: Germany is Freedom, we and Polynesia and Egypt are Order, Aztecs are Autocracy, and Portugal, as a vassal of the Aztecs, has no listed ideology. My priority is preventing Germany's diplomatic victory by disrupting their delegate count in the World Congress, especially since the vote is in three turns. I'm also assessing our tactical fronts, specifically Lisbon's precarious state, as the Aztecs are heavily reinforcing it.
            
            **Reinforcing Critical Fronts**
            
            I'm assessing the overwhelming Aztec force concentration near Lisbon and Braga. Our current research focus is on Nuclear Fission, but the imminent threat necessitates a re-evaluation of military priorities and potential unit construction to bolster our defenses.
            
            **Researching Key Technologies**
            
            I'm reviewing our technology path. While Nuclear Fission progresses, I'm confirming that Advanced Ballistics is the next logical step, unlocking crucial offensive and scientific victory capabilities. Refrigeration also remains an option.
            
            **Prioritizing Research Pathways**
            
            I'm confirming that Advanced Ballistics remains our next research target, crucial for unlocking Nuclear Missiles post-Nuclear Fission. I'm also evaluating Order ideology tenets like 'Dictatorship of the Proletariat' or 'Five-Year Plan' for our next policy adoption.
            
            **Prioritizing Technology Acquisition**
            
            I'm prioritizing Great Leap Forward for the free technology it grants. This will significantly accelerate our research towards Advanced Ballistics and its subsequent unlock of Nuclear Missiles, a critical strategic advantage in the upcoming turns.
            
            **Adopting Strategic Tenets**
            
            I'm evaluating Order ideology's Level 2 tenets. Great Leap Forward offers a free technology, accelerating my path to Advanced Ballistics and Nuclear Missiles, which is vital given the ongoing war and looming World Congress vote.
            
            **Selecting Policy Tenets**
            
            I'm confirming that "Great Leap Forward" is the optimal choice for policy adoption. The free technology it provides will significantly boost my science, accelerating critical research for nuclear capabilities and missile development, which is paramount given the current geopolitical climate and my need to disrupt Germany's diplomatic victory path.
            
            **Defending Vital Territories**
            
            I'm now intensely focused on the looming threat to Lisbon and Porto. Polynesia's military is engaging the Aztecs, which is a welcome distraction, but I must reinforce my own cities. Lisbon's low health is a major concern, and the potential for the Aztecs to gain domination victory by capturing it requires my immediate attention.
            
            **Securing Strategic Assets**
            
            I'm re-evaluating my resource allocation. With Manhattan Project complete and two Uranium available, I can now build atomic bombs or missiles once the necessary technologies are researched. My focus remains on securing Lisbon and Porto from the Aztec threat, while still preparing for offensive capabilities.
            
            **Prioritizing Atomic Capabilities**
            
            I'm now able to construct two atomic bombs, a crucial step toward realizing my nuclear ambitions. My immediate focus shifts to rapid scientific advancement, prioritizing Nuclear Fission and Advanced Ballistics to unlock missile capabilities and secure victory.
            
            **Disrupting German Victory**
            
            I'm now assessing Germany's delegate count and their path to victory. With only three turns until the World Congress vote, I must disrupt their lead, especially since their peace treaty with Polynesia expires simultaneously. I'm scrutinizing their city-state alliances for potential diplomatic shifts.
            
            **Disrupting Ideological Control**
            
            I'm analyzing the World Congress proposals and realizing a significant opportunity. With the non-Freedom bloc united against Germany's ideology, I see a clear path to disrupting their delegate lead and preventing their victory, especially with the upcoming vote.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            The non-Freedom coalition has enough votes to repeal Germany's World Ideology, blocking their diplomatic victory. I need to ensure I vote to repeal and maintain our strong alliance with Polynesia.
            
            **Strengthening Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm focusing on solidifying my alliances. I will set Polynesia's relations to maximum to ensure our vital defensive pact and trade routes remain secure against the Aztec threat. Egypt's relations are already strong, and I'm considering minor adjustments while keeping them high. Germany's relations remain neutral for now.
            
            **Forcing Diplomatic Capitulation**
            
            I'm now escalating hostilities with Portugal, setting our relationship to hostile to provoke their immediate elimination by the Aztecs. This strategic move aims to strip the enemy coalition of their delegates and redirect our naval forces to confront the Aztec threat head-on.
            
            **Selecting Next Policy**
            
            I am confirming that "Great Leap Forward" remains the optimal policy choice. It will grant a free technology, significantly accelerating my science towards Advanced Ballistics and Nuclear Missiles, which is critical given the current geopolitical climate and my need to disrupt Germany's diplomatic victory.
            
            **Researching Key Advancements**
            
            I'm confirming "Great Leap Forward" as the optimal next policy for the free technology, accelerating my path to Advanced Ballistics and ultimately Nuclear Missiles. I'm also verifying that Advanced Ballistics remains the correct next research target post-Nuclear Fission.
            
            **Adjusting Strategic Flavors**
            
            I'm recalibrating my strategic priorities. With the Manhattan Project concluded and nuclear capabilities within reach, I'm emphasizing defense and city resilience to hold key locations like Lisbon and Porto against the Aztec onslaught. Simultaneously, I'm priming for immediate nuclear deployment upon researching the necessary technologies.
            
            **Bolstering Aerial Defenses**
            
            I'm prioritizing anti-air capabilities due to the significant Aztec air presence. Bazookas, fighters, and mobile units are being reinforced to protect our cities and crucial research efforts towards Nuclear Fission and Advanced Ballistics, while maintaining enough offensive pressure to take Funchal.
            
            **Verifying Strategic Inputs**
            
            I'm meticulously cross-referencing all executed commands, ensuring `Advanced Ballistics` is correctly set for research and `Great Leap Forward` for policy. I'm also confirming Polynesia remains at max relations and Portugal is set to hostile, aiming to provoke their swift demise.
            
            **Executing Diplomatic Maneuvers**
            
            I am focused on eliminating Portugal swiftly to remove their delegates from the enemy coalition, thereby freeing up naval assets for the ongoing conflict with the Aztecs. My research priorities are confirmed: Advanced Ballistics is next, leading directly to Nuclear Missiles. I'm switching policy to Great Leap Forward for its free technology to accelerate this path, and bolstering city defenses while preparing for immediate nuclear deployment.

            - Rationale: PORTUGAL ELIMINATION MANDATE: Funchal is at 5% health and 9 defense strength. Eliminate Portugal immediately to remove their 6 delegates from the enemy coalition and free up our naval units for the Aztec front.

          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: DgsqfrlA9YZ79FYg
          - toolName: set-research
          - input:
            - Technology: Advanced Ballistics
            - Rationale: NUCLEAR MISSILE PATH: Confirming Advanced Ballistics as next tech. Once Nuclear Fission is complete in 10 turns, Advanced Ballistics unlocks Nuclear Missiles (and SS Booster spaceship parts). This is our path to ultimate deterrence and a backup science victory.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #4:

          - toolName: set-policy

            - Rationale: GREAT LEAP FORWARD FREE TECH: Switching next policy to Great Leap Forward. The free technology will instantly complete Advanced Ballistics or another high-tier tech, saving us crucial turns in the nuclear race, while also boosting spy science.
            - Policy: Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order)
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #5:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: dsdcoh5kpnxPpDlx
          - toolName: set-flavors
          - input:
            - Rationale: MANHATTAN PROJECT ACTIVE - DEFENSIVE DETERRENCE: Manhattan Project is complete! Maintain max Defense (100), CityDefense (100), Mobilization (100), and Antiair (100) to hold Lisbon (7% health) and Porto. Set Nuke and UseNuke to 100 to build and deploy atomic weapons immediately upon unlocking the tech. Max Science (100) and Production (100) to speed up research and unit construction. Offense 40 to encourage capturing Funchal (5% health) while avoiding suicidal head-on attacks against the main Aztec army.
            - Flavors:
              - Production: 100
              - Spaceship: 85
              - Defense: 100
              - Espionage: 80
              - Gold: 40
              - UseNuke: 100
              - Mobilization: 100
              - Mobile: 80

              - Nuke: 100
              - CityDefense: 100
              - Air: 90
              - Ranged: 95
